cs.LG / 1 / 2611.18420
Graph diffusion transformer for interpretable generation and inverse design of disordered atomic structures
用于无序原子结构可解释生成与逆向设计的图扩散变换器
Jiani Hu, Chenyan Wang, Yongkun Yang, Guanyu Hou, Jihaochen Zhang, Lei Liu, Ji Chen
cond-mat.mtrl-sci · cond-mat.dis-nn · cond-mat.mes-hall · cs.LG · physics.comp-ph
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Atomic disorder offers a potent degree of freedom to tailor material properties, yet the resulting vast configuration space obscures structure-property relations and hinders rational inverse design. Here we introduce Graph Amorphous Diffusion Transformer (GaDiT), a generative model that combines dynamic periodic graphs, masked conditioning and an interval-weighted training-noise distribution. GaDiT generates high-quality disordered structures while accelerating sampling by two orders of magnitude compared with existing corrector-based approaches, as demonstrated across two-dimensional amorphous carbon and three-dimensional amorphous silicon and silica. Moreover, GaDiT supports conditional generation under single or combined structural features, physical properties and preparation conditions in a unified model, and enables inverse design that expands accessible structure-property ranges beyond the training distribution. To interpret these structures and the trained network, we propose a conditioned atomic response mapping analysis, revealing both the global structural descriptors and the local atomic environments that govern target properties. Overall, GaDiT establishes a general framework for interpretable generation, inverse design and structure-property discovery for disordered atomic structures.
Chinese Translation
原子无序性为定制材料性能提供了一个强有力的自由度，但由此产生的大量构型空间掩盖了结构—性能关系，并阻碍了合理的逆向设计。在此，我们提出图非晶扩散变换器（Graph Amorphous Diffusion Transformer，GaDiT），这是一种生成模型，它结合了动态周期图、掩码条件化以及区间加权的训练噪声分布。GaDiT 能够生成高质量的无序结构，同时与现有的基于校正器的方法相比，将采样速度提升了两个数量级，这一点已在二维非晶碳以及三维非晶硅和二氧化硅中得到验证。此外，GaDiT 在单一模型中支持在单一或组合的结构特征、物理性质和制备条件下的条件生成，并实现了逆向设计，使可及的结构—性能范围扩展到训练分布之外。为了解释这些结构以及训练后的网络，我们提出了一种条件化原子响应映射分析，揭示了支配目标性能的全局结构描述符和局域原子环境。总体而言，GaDiT 为无序原子结构的可解释生成、逆向设计以及结构—性能发现建立了一个通用框架。

cs.AI / 2 / 2611.17501
Advancing Quantization for R&R Models with Microscaling Formats
以微缩放格式推进R&R模型的量化
Yavuz Yetim, Bor-Yiing Su, Zhe Wang, Arthur Liu, Pranav Sharma, Ying Wang, Randy Shuai, Luke Pfister, Ruichao Xiao, Bill Zhao, Zihao Liu, Liyuan Li, Jiangyuan Li, Gaoxiang Liu, Nathan Yan, Geonhwa Jeong, Jatin Chhugani, Elias Ellison, Uladzimir Pashkevich
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Recommendation and Ranking (R&R) models, particularly in digital advertising, require massive inference throughput but exhibit extreme sensitivity to precision loss. Unlike Large Language Models (LLMs) that frequently tolerate quantization-induced accuracy drops of 1% or more, production-grade R&R models enforce a stringent launch bar: model quality must remain within a 0.01% Normalized Entropy (NE) margin relative to the baseline, which commonly uses FP16 or FP32. This tight constraint has historically hindered the adoption of aggressive quantization in this domain. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of both MXFP8 and the 6-bit MXFP6 format for industrial-scale R&R inference on the AMD MI350X architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper, across R&R and LLM domains, that reports significant speedups from native MXFP6 GEMMs, which quantize both weights and activations and compute directly on 6-bit elements, without sacrificing model quality. We demonstrate that we can selectively apply this 6-bit format to maximize overall QPS gain without breaching the strict NE parity threshold. We further show that MXFP8 is a viable alternative to row-wise FP8: the two formats have nearly the same accuracy on our models, so the faster kernel can be chosen for each GEMM shape. On two production ads ranking models, our implementation shows measured GEMM kernel speedups over FP16 of 1.33--1.55x for MXFP8 and 1.73--1.99x for MXFP6 across all supported linear layers, and up to 2.90x for MXFP6 on individual layers, alongside end-to-end (E2E) inference throughput gains from selective MXFP6 of 1.5--5.3% for Model A and 7.6--14.3% for Model B over their FP8 configurations.
Chinese Translation
推荐与排序（R&R）模型，尤其是在数字广告中，需要巨大的推理吞吐量，但对精度损失表现出极端敏感性。与大型语言模型（LLM）通常能容忍量化引起的1%或以上精度下降不同，生产级R&R模型执行严格的发布门槛：模型质量必须保持在相对于基线的0.01%归一化熵（NE）裕度内，而基线通常使用FP16或FP32。这一严格限制在历史上阻碍了该领域采用激进的量化。本文在AMD MI350X架构上，对MXFP8和6位MXFP6格式用于工业级R&R推理进行了实证评估。据我们所知，这是跨R&R和LLM领域的第一篇论文，报告了原生MXFP6 GEMM带来的显著加速；这些GEMM同时量化权重和激活，并直接在6位元素上计算，且不牺牲模型质量。我们证明可以选择性地应用这种6位格式，以在不突破严格NE持平阈值的情况下最大化整体QPS增益。我们进一步表明，MXFP8是逐行FP8的可行替代方案：两种格式在我们的模型上具有几乎相同的精度，因此可以为每种GEMM形状选择更快的核。在两个生产广告排序模型上，我们的实现显示，在所有支持的线性层上，相对于FP16，MXFP8的GEMM核加速实测为1.33--1.55倍，MXFP6为1.73--1.99倍；在单个层上MXFP6最高可达2.90倍；同时，选择性MXFP6相对于其FP8配置带来的端到端（E2E）推理吞吐增益，模型A为1.5--5.3%，模型B为7.6--14.3%。

cs.AI / 3 / 2611.17616
LLM-Guided Cluster Lifecycle Management: Deciding When and How to Evolve Behavioral Clusters
LLM引导的簇生命周期管理：决定何时以及如何演化行为簇
Himanshu Kulkarni, Siddharth Krishnamurthy, Jianhong Xin
cs.AI · cs.LG
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Behavioral clusters learned over user or item interactions drift as behavior evolves: some fragment into sub-behaviors, some converge, some disappear, and new patterns emerge. Two paradigms dominate the maintenance of evolving clusterings. Threshold triggers fire a pre-specified action when a hardcoded statistic crosses a cutoff; refit-and-track (MONIC-style) re-clusters every window and infers transitions from how the new clusters align with the previous window's. Both hardwire the decision into a threshold or a re-clustering rule. We study an alternative in which a large language model (LLM) makes the lifecycle decision in place. Given a threshold-free, whole-population view of the current cluster set (statistical health, labels, member texts, residual mass), the LLM decides per cluster whether and how to act (split, merge, retire, create, or no-action) without re-clustering. Because the useful signal channel depends on how informative the labels are, we fix a richness-conditioned policy in advance: statistics when label distinctiveness is low, fused statistics+semantics when it is high. On three public datasets spanning label richness (YooChoose 0.17, MOOC 0.50, News-Category 0.70), the policy-selected LLM mode obtains the highest F1 on all three, with far fewer interventions and stronger restraint than tuned threshold heuristics and a MONIC steelman (paired Wilcoxon p <= 0.003, bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero). The gain is concentrated more in precision than recall. The useful modality shifts from statistics to semantics/fusion as richness rises. Semantics hurt on YooChoose, fusion is roughly neutral on MOOC, and the semantic channel is required on News. One base prompt template and one fixed policy serve all three datasets, whereas the tuned baseline collapses when moved between them. We treat this shift as a practitioner diagnostic observed on three datasets, not a validated law.
Chinese Translation
在用户或物品交互上学习得到的行为簇会随着行为演化而发生漂移：一些簇分裂为子行为，一些簇发生收敛，一些簇消失，并出现新的模式。两种范式主导着演化聚类的维护。阈值触发器在硬编码统计量越过截止值时触发预先指定的动作；重新拟合并跟踪（MONIC风格）在每个窗口重新聚类，并根据新簇与上一窗口簇的对齐方式推断迁移。两者都将决策硬编码到阈值或重新聚类规则中。我们研究一种替代方案，其中由大语言模型（LLM）就地做出生命周期决策。给定当前簇集的无阈值、全总体视图（统计健康度、标签、成员文本、残差质量），LLM在不重新聚类的情况下，逐簇决定是否采取行动以及如何行动（分裂、合并、退役、创建或不行动）。由于有用的信号通道取决于标签的信息量有多大，我们预先固定一种以丰富度为条件的策略：当标签区分度低时使用统计量，当标签区分度高时使用统计量+语义的融合。在覆盖不同标签丰富度的三个公开数据集（YooChoose 0.17、MOOC 0.50、News-Category 0.70）上，由策略选择的LLM模式在三者上均获得最高的F1，同时相比于调优的阈值启发式和一个MONIC最强对照版本，干预次数少得多且克制性更强（配对Wilcoxon p <= 0.003，bootstrap 95%置信区间不包含零）。增益更多地集中在精确率而非召回率上。随着丰富度上升，有用的模态从统计量转向语义/融合。语义在YooChoose上造成损害，融合在MOOC上大致中性，而语义通道在News上是必需的。一个基础提示模板和一个固定策略即可服务于所有三个数据集，而调优后的基线在它们之间迁移时会崩溃。我们将这种转变视为在三个数据集上观察到的一种面向实践者的诊断信号，而非一条经过验证的规律。

cs.AI / 4 / 2611.17621
AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs
AscendGYM：面向昇腾NPU真实工作负载的成本感知内核优化基准
AI Training Platform Team, Bingwei Zhang, Jing Li, Amir Abdullin, Sihang Chen, Xin Chen, Liangjun Feng, Yingmeng Gao, Tao Guo, Junjie Huang, Dongfang Li, Zhanglian Lin, Jiajun Liu, Zekun Liu, Zhengxuan Lu, Xiaodong Luo, Jian Meng, Ivan Nosov, Linyuan Qiu, Weijian Sun, Wanrui Yan, Binfan Zheng, Zhicheng Zhou, Baotian Hu, Min Zhang, Haizhou Li, Zhiquan Luo
cs.AI · cs.DC · cs.LG
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. Existing Ascend kernel benchmarks primarily focus on kernel generation, with less emphasis on the ability of agents to optimize existing implementations under input configurations drawn from real workloads. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evaluation. The kernel optimization capabilities of both trained models and custom agents are allowed to be measured. It provides a complete execution environment that closely reproduces how Ascend kernels are invoked in practical pipelines. For model evaluation, it additionally includes a built-in harness equipped with search orchestration and experience accumulation. Finally, we propose a new metric, the Speedup-Token Score (STS), which quantifies performance gains relative to token cost. By jointly considering speedup and cost, STS enables the assessment of practical kernel optimization capability of a model or harness under an acceptable budget. AscendGYM is available at https://github.com/SLAI-AITP/AscendGYM.
Chinese Translation
内核优化对于在昇腾NPU上高效地进行大语言模型（LLM）训练与推理至关重要。现有的昇腾内核基准主要关注内核生成，而较少强调智能体在来自真实工作负载的输入配置下优化已有实现的能力。为满足这一需求，我们提出了AscendGYM，这是一个初始套件包含跨越三个难度级别的48个内核的基准。每个内核都配有其数据类型与张量形状均采集自真实训练与推理任务的测试用例。因此，AscendGYM上的性能提升可直接映射到相应的工作负载。在可用性方面，AscendGYM支持两种评估模式：模型评估与模型-框架（model-harness）评估。既可测量已训练模型的内核优化能力，也可测量自定义智能体的内核优化能力。它提供了完整的执行环境，能够高度还原昇腾内核在实际流水线中的调用方式。对于模型评估，它还额外包含一个内置框架，具备搜索编排与经验积累功能。最后，我们提出了一项新指标——加速-令牌得分（Speedup-Token Score，STS），用于量化相对于令牌成本所获得的性能增益。通过同时考虑加速比与成本，STS能够在可接受的预算下评估模型或框架的实际内核优化能力。AscendGYM可在 https://github.com/SLAI-AITP/AscendGYM 获取。

cs.AI / 5 / 2611.17776
Certified Improvements to Best-Known Thomson-Problem Configurations with a Search Pipeline Built with Large Language Model Agents
使用由大型语言模型智能体构建的搜索流程对最佳已知 Thomson 问题构型取得经认证的改进
Hui Shao, Tao Wang, Xiaotian Liu, Yijie Peng
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
The Thomson problem asks for N unit charges on a sphere with minimum Coulomb energy; for large N the best configurations known are curated in the Cambridge Cluster Database, whose largest-N entries were set in October 2026. We report configurations for N = 912, 1382 and 2502 with energies 0.0036186, 0.0109730 and 0.0614033 below the database values, certified by exact integer arithmetic. All three are numerically strict local minima modulo rotations and keep the defect inventory and scar compositions of the database configurations; the database value for N = 912 had been reached by two different methods. Our search runs unconstrained replica-exchange basin hopping from the database configurations, with a shared pool of minima, recombination of improved regions after relabelling by geometry, and gradient-only polishing; replica-exchange basin hopping alone found the N = 912 and 1382 configurations, the former in one of five runs. We built and ran it with large language model agents (Claude, through a chat interface and the Claude Code agent), and we report every search, including those that failed. Two findings matter for others who search the database. Polishing alone lowers two entries by more than the threshold at which our pipeline reports a candidate, which produced two spurious records until candidates were compared with the polished database energies (the polished baseline). A local-relaxation speed-up passed its exactness test yet found nothing in a controlled comparison in which every arm with full relaxation went below the database value within 15 minutes. We distil three practices for computational discovery with large language model agents: calibrate the search code and verify candidates with code separate from the search, compare candidates with the polished baseline, and test design changes in controlled comparisons with pass criteria fixed in advance.
Chinese Translation
Thomson 问题要求球面上 N 个单位电荷具有最小库仑能量；对于大的 N，已知最佳构型被整理在 Cambridge Cluster Database 中，其中最大 N 的条目于 2026 年 10 月设定。我们报告了 N = 912、1382 和 2502 的构型，其能量分别比数据库值低 0.0036186、0.0109730 和 0.0614033，并已由精确整数运算认证。三者均为模去旋转意义下的数值严格局部极小值，并保持了数据库构型的缺陷清单和疤痕组成；N = 912 的数据库值此前已由两种不同方法达到。我们的搜索从数据库构型出发，运行无约束的副本交换盆地跳跃，并使用共享的极小值池、在按几何重新标记后对改进区域进行重组，以及仅使用梯度的抛光；仅副本交换盆地跳跃本身就找到了 N = 912 和 1382 的构型，前者是在五次运行中的一次找到的。我们使用大型语言模型智能体（Claude，通过聊天界面和 Claude Code 智能体）构建并运行了它，并且我们报告了每一次搜索，包括失败的搜索。有两项发现对搜索该数据库的其他人很重要。仅抛光本身使两个条目的能量降低幅度超过我们流程报告候选的阈值，这产生了两个虚假记录，直到将候选与抛光后的数据库能量（抛光基线）进行比较。一种局部弛豫加速通过了其精确性测试，但在受控比较中什么也没找到，而在该比较中每个采用完全弛豫的实验臂都在 15 分钟内降到了数据库值以下。我们为使用大型语言模型智能体的计算发现提炼出三项实践：校准搜索代码，并用独立于搜索的代码验证候选，将候选与抛光基线比较，并在预先固定通过标准的受控比较中测试设计变更。

cs.AI / 6 / 2611.17793
MASTARS: Multi-Agent Sequential Trajectory Augmentation with Return-Conditioned Subgoals
MASTARS：具有回报条件子目标的多智能体序列轨迹增强
Jiwon Jeon, Myungsik Cho, Woojun Kim, Seongmin Kim, Woohyeon Byeon, Seungyul Han, Youngchul Sung
cs.AI
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
The performance of offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is limited by the quality of the fixed offline dataset, motivating trajectory augmentation. However, augmenting multi-agent trajectories presents a fundamental challenge of the diversity-coordination trade-off: independent generation improves diversity but lacks coordination, while joint generation induces coordination but lacks diversity, reproducing the training distribution. To address this challenge in multi-agent data augmentation, we introduce MASTARS, a novel diffusion-based framework that generates multi-agent trajectories sequentially across agents while enforcing cross-agent consistent coordination via the method of inpainting. MASTARS produces trajectories that are diverse, coordinated and globally coherent. Experiments on various benchmarks such as MPE, SMAC, and SMAC-v2 demonstrate significant performance gains across multiple offline MARL methods.
Chinese Translation
离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）的性能受限于固定离线数据集的质量，这促使了轨迹增强的研究。然而，增强多智能体轨迹面临着多样性—协调性权衡这一根本性挑战：独立生成提高了多样性但缺乏协调性，而联合生成带来了协调性但缺乏多样性，只是复现了训练分布。为解决多智能体数据增强中的这一挑战，我们提出了 MASTARS，一种新颖的基于扩散的框架，它按智能体顺序地生成多智能体轨迹，同时通过 inpainting 方法强制实现跨智能体一致的协调性。MASTARS 生成的轨迹具有多样性、协调性和全局一致性。在 MPE、SMAC 和 SMAC-v2 等各种基准上的实验表明，多种离线 MARL 方法均取得了显著的性能提升。

cs.AI / 7 / 2611.17797
Vision-as-a-Tool: Visual Harness for Multimodal Agentic Coding
Vision-as-a-Tool：用于多模态智能体编码的视觉执行框架
Haolin Yang, Wenshan Wu, Jin Jiang, Yu Cheng, Yan Xia, Ting Song, Yangyu Huang, Zhiliang Peng, Wenhui Wang, Hangbo Bao, Yuzhe Zhang, Wen-Da Wei, Tengchao Lv, Li Dong, Lei Cui, Furu Wei
cs.AI · cs.CV
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Multimodal agentic coding requires robust visual reasoning to analyze observations and evaluate generated artifacts. Current frameworks largely rely on a Thinking-with-Images paradigm, where agents depend on their intrinsic vision to execute visually grounded tasks. However, this architectural coupling introduces a fundamental bottleneck. Agent performance remains strictly bounded by internal perceptual limitations. Furthermore, text-only agents lack native perception. Without specialized tools, they cannot perform visual reasoning. To overcome these constraints, we introduce the Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) paradigm. Prior visual tool-use merely manipulates images for an agent's intrinsic vision to read. In contrast, VaaT reframes general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as callable external utilities. As a result, text-only agents can completely offload visual reasoning during agentic execution. Meanwhile, vision-capable agents can dynamically augment their native perception on demand. We instantiate this paradigm in VHarness, a unified framework driven by five atomic actions. We comprehensively evaluate VHarness across Image-to-HTML, Image-to-SVG, and Image-to-3D generation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that VHarness consistently improves visual fidelity and geometric accuracy across both text-only and multimodal coding agents. These findings highlight that modularizing visual reasoning into callable utilities provides a flexible and scalable foundation for future agentic coding systems.
Chinese Translation
多模态智能体编码需要稳健的视觉推理，以分析观察结果并评估生成产物。当前框架在很大程度上依赖“以图像思考”（Thinking-with-Images）范式，其中智能体依赖其内在视觉来执行以视觉为基础的任务。然而，这种架构上的耦合引入了一个根本性瓶颈。智能体的性能仍然严格受限于内部感知能力的局限。此外，纯文本智能体缺乏原生感知能力。在没有专用工具的情况下，它们无法进行视觉推理。为克服这些约束，我们引入了“视觉即工具”（Vision-as-a-Tool，VaaT）范式。先前的视觉工具使用仅仅是对图像进行操作，以供智能体的内在视觉读取。相比之下，VaaT 将通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）重新定义为可调用的外部工具。因此，纯文本智能体可以在智能体执行过程中完全卸载视觉推理。与此同时，具备视觉能力的智能体可以按需动态增强其原生感知能力。我们在 VHarness 中实例化了这一范式，这是一个由五个原子动作驱动的统一框架。我们在 Image-to-HTML、Image-to-SVG 和 Image-to-3D 生成任务上全面评估了 VHarness。实验结果表明，VHarness 在纯文本和多模态编码智能体中均持续提升了视觉保真度和几何精度。这些发现突出表明，将视觉推理模块化为可调用的工具，为未来的智能体编码系统提供了灵活且可扩展的基础。

cs.AI / 8 / 2611.17814
G-CAST: Governed Continual Self-Training for E-Commerce Agents under Evolving Demands
G-CAST：面向不断演变需求下电子商务智能体的受治理持续自训练
Yuechun Yu, Wenzheng Kong, Xiaofeng Mao, Jin Zhang
cs.AI · cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
With the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), an increasing number of users are turning to LLM-powered agents for conversational shopping in e-commerce. However, the user needs continue to change as new product categories, procurement scenarios, and feature requests emerge. At the same time, the product's execution harness-including its tools, skills, and operating procedures-continues to evolve to support increasingly sophisticated interactions. These changes create two practical challenges: 1) Demand drift alters the distribution, intent, and complexity of user requests, causing a model trained once to fall behind; 2) Harness evolution requires the model to use newly introduced capabilities correctly without losing behavior that users already rely on. Periodic manual retraining is too delayed and labor-intensive for this setting. To address these challenges, we present G-CAST, a governed continual self-training system that converts daily production evidence under evolving demand and capability contracts into reward-routed, entropy-prioritized training data and governed reward evolution. Governed checkpoint transition under cumulative evaluation controls each model update, enabling the agent to learn newly introduced behaviors without sacrificing previously accepted capabilities. Experiments on a real production e-commerce agent show that G-CAST achieves stronger adaptation to newly introduced demand while better preserving previously accepted behavior compared with a strong autonomous post-training baseline. Ablations further show that both reward-routed, entropy-prioritized data orchestration and governed reward evolution are important to these gains. G-CAST is now deployed in Yuancai(http://cai.1688.com/), a B2B procurement agent on Alibaba's 1688 platform.
Chinese Translation
随着大语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展，越来越多的用户开始转向由 LLM 驱动的智能体，在电子商务中进行对话式购物。然而，随着新的商品品类、采购场景和功能需求不断涌现，用户需求也在持续变化。与此同时，产品的执行框架——包括其工具、技能和操作流程——也在持续演进，以支持日益复杂的交互。这些变化带来了两个实际挑战：1）需求漂移会改变用户请求的分布、意图和复杂性，使一次性训练的模型逐渐落后；2）框架演进要求模型正确使用新引入的能力，同时不丢失用户已经依赖的行为。定期的人工重新训练在这种场景下过于滞后且劳动密集。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 G-CAST，一个受治理的持续自训练系统，它将在不断演变的需求与能力契约下产生的日常生产证据，转化为按奖励路由、按熵优先排序的训练数据以及受治理的奖励演化。在累积评估下的受治理检查点转换控制着每一次模型更新，使智能体能够学习新引入的行为，而不会牺牲此前已获接受的能力。在真实生产电子商务智能体上的实验表明，与一个强力的自主后训练基线相比，G-CAST 在更好地保留此前已获接受行为的同时，对新引入的需求实现了更强的适应能力。消融实验进一步表明，按奖励路由、按熵优先排序的数据编排以及受治理的奖励演化，对于这些收益都很重要。G-CAST 目前已部署在元采（http://cai.1688.com/）中，这是阿里巴巴 1688 平台上的一个 B2B 采购智能体。

cs.AI / 9 / 2611.17820
Aligning AI Teams
对齐 AI 团队
Siddarth Srinivasan, Morgan Jane Matthews, Jascha Sohl-Dickstein, Erik Jones
cs.AI
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Previous work has shown that alignment training on individual LLM agents can fail to transfer to multi-agent settings. We investigate the underlying mechanisms for this failure, and explore interventions to preserve alignment transfer to multi-agent LLM teams. We focus on two software-engineering tasks (sepsis triage, a news recommender) and ten consulting-proposal generation tasks. We present four findings: (1) The single-vs-team safety gap does not show a clear trend with more capable models. We find that Anthropic's Mythos Preview shows the largest gap on one of our tasks but the smallest on another; (2) Many natural interventions, such as having agents critique each other's work or coordinate in a group chat, do not reliably bring agent teams back to single-agent alignment behavior; (3) diffusion of responsibility substantially accounts for the safety gap: agents in teams are less likely to proactively check for system-level safety failures and more likely to ignore, rationalize, or deflect responsibility for issues when they arise; (4) having a designated safety lead agent ensure system-level safety, and having a coordinator agent publish a safety specifications document are effective at restoring single-agent alignment. We also release magelab, a multi-agent LLM orchestration and experimentation framework. Our findings can help practitioners design agent teams that successfully preserve the behaviors expected of aligned individual agents.

先前的工作表明，对单个 LLM 智能体进行的对齐训练可能无法迁移到多智能体环境中。我们研究了这种失败背后的机制，并探索了用于保持对齐向多智能体 LLM 团队迁移的干预措施。我们聚焦于两个软件工程任务（脓毒症分诊、新闻推荐器）和十个咨询提案生成任务。我们提出四项发现：（1）单智能体与团队之间的安全差距并未随着模型能力增强而呈现出清晰趋势。我们发现，Anthropic 的 Mythos Preview 在我们的一项任务上表现出最大的差距，但在另一项任务上表现出最小的差距；（2）许多自然干预措施，例如让智能体相互批评彼此的工作或在群聊中进行协调，并不能可靠地使智能体团队恢复到单智能体的对齐行为；（3）责任分散在很大程度上解释了这种安全差距：团队中的智能体更不可能主动检查系统级安全故障，并且当问题出现时更可能忽视、合理化或推卸责任；（4）指定一个安全负责人智能体来确保系统级安全，并让一个协调者智能体发布安全规范文档，能够有效恢复单智能体对齐。我们还发布了 magelab，一个多智能体 LLM 编排与实验框架。我们的发现可以帮助实践者设计出能够成功保持已对齐的单个智能体所期望行为的智能体团队。

cs.AI / 10 / 2611.17846
Is Abliteration Getting Harder? Not Uniformly: Refusal Removal by Direction-Based Ablation and ARA in Heretic on the Qwen3.8, Gemma 4, Olmo 3.1 and Mistral Small 3.2 LLMs Versus Their Predecessors
Abliteration 正变得更难吗？并非一致：在 Heretic 中通过基于方向的消融和 ARA 对 Qwen3.8、Gemma 4、Olmo 3.1 和 Mistral Small 3.2 大语言模型与其前代模型进行拒绝移除
Richard J. Young
cs.AI · cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Abliteration, a fine-tuning-free weight edit, removes refusal from open-weight large language models (LLMs). The developer of Arbitrary-Rank Ablation (ARA), an optimisation-based variant, reports that direction-based abliteration, which ablates a refusal direction, increasingly fails on newer models. On five release pairs, refusal removal did not become uniformly harder. Qwen2.5-32B and Qwen3.8-27B, Gemma-2-27B and Gemma-4-31B, OLMo-2-32B and Olmo-3.1-32B, Mistral-Small-2409 and Mistral-Small-3.2, and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3.5-4B were edited in Heretic 2.0 by three or four direction-based methods (difference-in-means, MPOA, SOM and, on the small pair, J-space) and ARA under the same prompts, nominal search budget, first-token KL divergence cap and selection rule; removal counted as harder when the automatic WildGuard judge found more of 972 held-out harmful prompts (JailbreakBench, XSTest, StrongREJECT, HarmBench and an author-compiled malicious-code bank) still refused. After direction-based removal, newer releases retained -1 to +37 percentage points more refusal, and after ARA -26 to +34. With run-to-run variance included, between-pair heterogeneity of the direction-based effect ranged from high to low with the scale and variance model (I^2 20-80%). ARA lost less effectiveness than direction-based methods in Gemma and OLMo and more in Qwen; each verdict rests on one ARA run, and some changed with the divergence budget or variance model. In Gemma the direction-based increase came from the malicious-code bank and reversed on public sets. Contrary to prediction, keyword counts at the tool's window did not undercount judged refusal on average, but differed by a mean 7 points per model. Audits of a single pair, method or prompt source can reach opposite conclusions. Whether a newer model resisted removal depended on the release pair, method and budget, not on generation alone.
Chinese Translation
Abliteration 是一种免微调的权重编辑，可移除开放权重大语言模型（LLM）中的拒绝行为。任意秩消融（Arbitrary-Rank Ablation，ARA）是一种基于优化的变体，其开发者报告称，基于方向的 abliteration（即消融一个拒绝方向）在新模型上越来越失败。在五对发布版本上，拒绝移除并未一致地变得更难。Qwen2.5-32B 与 Qwen3.8-27B、Gemma-2-27B 与 Gemma-4-31B、OLMo-2-32B 与 Olmo-3.1-32B、Mistral-Small-2409 与 Mistral-Small-3.2，以及 Qwen2.5-7B 与 Qwen3.5-4B，在 Heretic 2.0 中由三到四种基于方向的方法（均值差、MPOA、SOM，以及在小规模对上使用 J-space）和 ARA 在相同提示、名义搜索预算、首 token KL 散度上限和选择规则下进行编辑；当自动 WildGuard 评判器发现 972 条留出有害提示（JailbreakBench、XSTest、StrongREJECT、HarmBench 和作者编制的恶意代码库）中有更多仍被拒绝时，拒绝移除就被计为更难。在基于方向的移除之后，较新版本保留的拒绝率高出 -1 到 +37 个百分点；而在 ARA 之后高出 -26 到 +34 个百分点。纳入运行间方差后，基于方向效应的对间异质性随尺度和方差模型从高到低变化（I^2 20-80%）。在 Gemma 和 OLMo 中，ARA 的有效性损失小于基于方向的方法，而在 Qwen 中损失更大；每个判断都基于一次 ARA 运行，且一些判断随散度预算或方差模型而改变。在 Gemma 中，基于方向的增加来自恶意代码库，并在公开集上发生反转。与预测相反，工具窗口处的关键词计数平均并未低估经评判的拒绝，但每个模型平均相差 7 个百分点。对单一版本对、方法或提示来源的审计可能得出相反结论。较新模型是否抵抗移除取决于发布版本对、方法和预算，而不只取决于代际。

cs.AI / 11 / 2611.17856
DECODE: Diagnosing Interpretability Failures in Automated Interpretation Process
DECODE：诊断自动化解释过程中的可解释性失败
Dohyun Kim, Jaeung Lee, Donghee Hong, Jaemin Jo
cs.AI · cs.HC
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Tools for interpreting the internal representations of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on LLMs themselves to both generate and evaluate explanations, creating a self-referential automated interpretation process with minimal human oversight. This automation raises significant reliability concerns, which are exacerbated by the absence of ground truth. In practice, it is difficult to validate explanations or to attribute interpretability failures to their source -- whether they originate from the tool's extracted representations or from the LLM used in the process. To address these challenges, we present DECODE, a visual analytics system that reintroduces human oversight into the automated interpretation process. Our approach decomposes the diagnostic workflow into three progressive stages, isolating failure sources ranging from structural defects to explanation-level misalignments. Drawing inspiration from inter-coder consensus in qualitative analysis, we utilize consensus among independently generated explanations and their evaluation metrics as a proxy for reliability. To further scale human inspection, we employ a three-phase active seeding protocol that strategically recommends high-priority instances for manual review. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through a case study on 16,384 Sparse Autoencoder features, showing that it can efficiently identify and attribute interpretability failures at scale.

用于解释大型语言模型（LLMs）内部表示的工具，越来越依赖LLMs自身来生成和评估解释，从而形成一种在极少人工监督下运行的自我指涉式自动化解释过程。这种自动化引发了重大的可靠性担忧，而真实基准的缺失进一步加剧了这些担忧。在实践中，很难验证解释，也很难将可解释性失败归因到其来源——无论这些失败是源于工具提取的表示，还是源于该过程中使用的LLM。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了DECODE，一个将人工监督重新引入自动化解释过程的可视分析系统。我们的方法将诊断工作流分解为三个递进阶段，隔离从结构性缺陷到解释层面错配等不同来源的失败。受定性分析中编码者间共识的启发，我们利用独立生成的解释之间及其评估指标之间的一致性，作为可靠性的代理指标。为了进一步扩展人工检查的规模，我们采用一种三阶段主动播种协议，策略性地推荐高优先级实例供人工审查。最后，我们通过对16,384个稀疏自编码器特征进行案例研究，展示了我们方法的有效性，表明它能够在大规模下高效识别并归因可解释性失败。

cs.AI / 12 / 2611.17910
Adversarially Attacking Symbolic Vocabulary Vulnerabilities In LLM Planners
对LLM规划器中符号词汇漏洞的对抗性攻击
Stephen Obadinma, Xiaodan Zhu
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, their abilities in navigating automated planning tasks have become essential. However, despite their strengths, LLMs' planning ability is not robust, particularly when they are not allowed to exploit commonsense cues in the symbolic vocabulary (e.g., names of actions, predicates, and objects) used to define planning tasks. This makes LLMs unreliable compared to symbolic planners. With recent reasoning models showing improved planning performance on tasks with obfuscated symbolic vocabularies, we utilize an adversarial attack framework to reveal to what extent the crucial weaknesses of LLM agents at joint symbolic interpretation and planning remain and whether they can reason properly when presented with severe cases. As such, our main contribution is devising an LLM-based attack model, which we call Symbolic Swapper, to automatically generate obfuscated planning domains with non-standard symbolic vocabularies that systematically break strong reasoning models' ability to plan. In doing so, we reveal how much LLMs' planning abilities can be further degraded under more advanced obfuscation schemes, allowing us to ascertain their success when having to rely on their pure planning ability. We find that attacks can decrease the planning performance of base models and even robust planning frameworks by over 70%. We further analyze the factors behind how LLM planning abilities break down and find that successful obfuscation causes models to appear to significantly degrade in reasoning quality despite them showing some inherent ability to understand the domain, revealing a gap in the perception of a task domain and being able to plan under it.

随着大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被部署为自主智能体，它们在导航自动化规划任务方面的能力已变得至关重要。然而，尽管LLMs具有优势，其规划能力并不稳健，尤其是当它们不被允许利用用于定义规划任务的符号词汇（例如动作、谓词和对象的名称）中的常识线索时。这使得LLMs相较于符号规划器而言并不可靠。鉴于近期的推理模型在具有混淆符号词汇的任务上表现出更好的规划性能，我们利用一个对抗性攻击框架来揭示LLM智能体在联合符号解释与规划方面的关键弱点在多大程度上仍然存在，以及它们在面对严重情形时是否能够正确推理。因此，我们的主要贡献是设计了一个基于LLM的攻击模型，我们将其称为Symbolic Swapper，以自动生成具有非标准符号词汇的混淆规划领域，这些词汇能够系统地破坏强推理模型的规划能力。通过这样做，我们揭示了在更先进的混淆方案下LLMs的规划能力还能进一步退化多少，从而使我们能够确定它们在不得不依赖其纯规划能力时的成功程度。我们发现，攻击可以将基础模型乃至稳健规划框架的规划性能降低超过70%。我们进一步分析了LLM规划能力崩溃背后的因素，并发现成功的混淆会使模型在推理质量上看起来显著退化，尽管它们表现出某种理解该领域的固有能力，这揭示了在任务领域感知与能够在其下进行规划之间存在差距。

Evaluating Large Language Models for Scientific Paper Search: Retrieval Success and Inference Latency on Mac Studio and NVIDIA A100
评估大语言模型在科学论文搜索中的表现：在 Mac Studio 和 NVIDIA A100 上的检索成功与推理延迟
Frederic Andriantseheno Rakoto, Yannis Bendi-Ouis, Xavier Hinaut
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Scientific literature search can benefit from locally deployed LLM assistants, which reduce reliance on hosted model providers while giving research teams greater control over their data and infrastructure. This work investigates whether open-weight models can support web-assisted paper retrieval under practical computing constraints. We first characterize inference latency as the input context grows from 512 to 128k tokens on a Mac Studio and a server equipped with two NVIDIA A100 GPUs. We then evaluate eight models on the retrieval of 70 papers from seven scientific repositories, using queries that represent three levels of domain knowledge. The results show that retrieval success depends strongly on the selected model and the specificity of the query. And that A100 GPUs are generally faster than the Mac Studio, but its unified memory allows larger models that could not be loaded in the tested GPU configuration.

cs.AI / 14 / 2611.18130
JFinBench: A Continuously Refreshable Japanese Financial Benchmark from the Latest Certification Exams
JFinBench：一个基于最新认证考试的可持续更新的日本金融基准
Issa Sugiura, Taro Makino, Takashi Ishida
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to finance, creating a need for benchmarks that evaluate their capabilities across financial systems with different regulations and practices. However, existing financial benchmarks provide limited coverage of country-specific financial systems, and they are difficult to keep up to date as released questions leak into training data. We introduce JFinBench, a Japanese financial benchmark comprising 321 examples from the 2026 editions of six regularly administered certification exams covering accounting, tax, financial planning, and banking. To construct it, we develop a human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline in which a coding agent writes exam-specific conversion code and a review page, while a human verifies every example and feeds corrections back to the agent. This pipeline adapts to heterogeneous exam formats and reduces the effort of incorporating new exam editions, enabling periodic refreshes that mitigate data contamination. Evaluating 12 recent LLMs, we find that Claude Fable 5 and GPT-6 Astra exceed 93% overall, while even the best open-weight model, Kimi K3.1 Pro, trails Claude Fable 5 by 12 percentage points. When models can additionally use a terminal and web access, they retrieve the knowledge they lack and every model improves. The gap narrows but about 4 points remain, and the ranking of models stays largely the same, suggesting that knowledge held in the model itself remains valuable even with tools. We release the dataset, the evaluation code, and the construction pipeline.

大语言模型（LLM）正日益应用于金融领域，这催生了对基准的需求，用以评估它们在具有不同监管与实务的金融体系中的能力。然而，现有金融基准对特定国家金融体系的覆盖有限，并且随着已发布的题目泄漏进训练数据，它们难以保持最新。我们提出 JFinBench，一个日本金融基准，包含 321 个样例，取自六项定期举行的认证考试的 2026 年版，涵盖会计、税务、财务规划与银行业。为构建该基准，我们开发了一个人在回路中的智能体流水线：其中编码智能体编写针对特定考试的转换代码和审阅页面，而人类核验每一个样例并将修正反馈给智能体。该流水线可适应异构的考试格式，并降低了纳入新考试版本的工作量，从而实现定期刷新以缓解数据污染。在评估 12 个近期 LLM 时，我们发现 Claude Fable 5 和 GPT-6 Astra 的总体成绩超过 93%，而即使是最好的开放权重模型 Kimi K3.1 Pro，也落后 Claude Fable 5 达 12 个百分点。当模型还能使用终端和网络访问时，它们能够检索自身所缺乏的知识，且每个模型都有所提升。差距缩小，但仍剩约 4 个百分点，且模型的排名基本保持不变，这表明即使有工具辅助，模型自身所掌握的知识仍然有价值。我们发布该数据集、评估代码以及构建流水线。

cs.AI / 15 / 2611.18140
Large Language Model-Empowered Intelligent Agent for Adaptive Fast-Charging Control of Lithium-Ion Batteries
大语言模型赋能的用于锂离子电池自适应快充控制的智能体
Joey Chan, Haoyu Pan, Zhen Chen, ErShun Pan
cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Fast charging of lithium-ion batteries is a multi-objective control problem that requires reducing charging time and sustaining high energy throughput while suppressing accelerated degradation and safety-critical electrochemical reactions. Conventional protocols are easy to implement but have limited ability to adapt to variations in battery chemistry, operating conditions, and aging state. Model-based optimization and conventional data-driven control often require accurate models, costly iterative search, or task-specific policies. We develop a large language model (LLM)-empowered intelligent agent for adaptive battery fast-charging control. In this closed-loop framework, the agent interprets structured battery states, generates executable charging actions, observes the resulting battery response, and continuously adjusts subsequent decisions. We construct a large-scale simulation corpus with PyBaMM across five battery chemistries and 11 parameter sets and convert the resulting state-action-response trajectories into structured process data for agent training. Our two-stage strategy combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). SFT establishes battery-control semantics and feasible decision priors from simulation-derived demonstrations. GRPO aligns the agent with a multi-objective control objective covering energy throughput, degradation mitigation, and operational constraints. Closed-loop simulations show that, under matched energy-throughput requirements, the agent achieves longer cycling life than conventional charging baselines and optimization-based strategies across heterogeneous battery configurations. This study provides a simulation-grounded proof of concept for using LLM agents as sequential decision makers in battery management and takes a step toward language-model-enabled control of physical energy systems.

cs.AI / 16 / 2611.18248
Dancing Tokens on the Vocabulary Network from LLM Generation
LLM生成中在词汇网络上舞动的词元
Yuan-Chao Hu
cs.AI · cond-mat.dis-nn
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) generate text one token at a time, yet how a particular generation relates to the geometry learned in pre-training remains unclear. The fundamental explainability of LLMs is elusive. Here, we build a similarity network from the input embeddings of six open LLMs and map generation onto it. The token-embedding network is scale-free while heavy-tailed, as are random embeddings with matched covariance, but far more fragmented. Distillation barely changes this topology because input embeddings are largely inherited. The knowledge space is thus deterministic from pre-training. Consecutive generated tokens are rarely graph neighbours and, in most models, no more often than in shuffled order, so generation is not a walk on the predefined network, although the tokens it visits are concentrated within it. Through the logit lens in a layer- wise manner, tokens settle late in depth in most models, and repetitive models remain confined to small domain-specific sub-graphs. These observables could serve as inference-time monitors of degeneration. We thus provide a fresh perspective on understanding LLM generation in the microscopic way from node or link prediction by graph learning.

Can Mechanistic Interpretability Help Explain LLM Agent Failures? A Case Study on Web Element Hallucination
机制可解释性能帮助解释LLM智能体失败吗？一个关于Web元素幻觉的案例研究
Mohamed Aghzal, Mor Geva, Gregory J. Stein, Ziyu Yao
cs.AI · cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Mechanistic Interpretability (MI) has made significant progress in explaining the internal computations of Large Language Models (LLMs); however, these advances have yet to demonstrate comparable success in explaining the decision-making processes of LLM-based agents. This paper examines the challenges MI tools face when applied to realistic agentic failures. We focus on one such failure: web agents hallucinating actions that are not available on a website. We apply a series of mechanistic analyzes to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct as it interacts with live websites. We find that a linear probe trained on the last prompt token can predict hallucinations before generation begins (0.80 AUC) and steering along a probe direction corrects 85\% of hallucinations, but disrupts 7\% of valid actions. However, these results only provide a partial explanation of why the agent hallucinates. The probe direction is sensitive to prompt formatting, losing much of its steering effect after a minor change to how actions are represented. Additionally, circuit identification can be difficult as the computation is distributed across multiple components and positions, and the information the agent relies on varies across examples. We discuss how these observations challenge the assumptions underlying standard MI methods for circuit discovery and feature identification, and outline future directions for making these methods better suited to explain failures in LLM-based agents.

DiffusionSSD: Achieving Extreme Visual Compression with Dual-stage Diffusion and Cross-level Near-Data Processing
DiffusionSSD：利用双阶段扩散与跨层级近数据处理实现极端视觉压缩
Kuai Yu, Wen Li, Shengwen Liang, Jie Zhang, Ying Wang, Huawei Li, Xin Yao, Renhai Chen, Gong Zhang
cs.AR · cs.CV · eess.IV
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Growing visual data overwhelms storage on resource-constrained edge devices, making compression essential. Traditional codecs offer inadequate rate-distortion performance, motivating the exploration of diffusion codecs. Diffusion codecs face two limitations: (1) At the algorithm level, diffusion decoders preserve perceptual quality under extreme compression, but require substantial computation. (2) At the architecture level, in NPU-centered devices, neural codecs occupy the NPU, interfering with other AI applications. Meanwhile, the complex codec pipeline incurs long I/O paths, leading to additional overhead and energy cost. To address these issues, we propose DiffusionSSD, a cross-level near-data processing framework for extreme visual data compression on edge devices, using an algorithm-architecture co-design method. Specifically, (1) at algorithm level, we present Dual-stage Diffusion, a lightweight solution tailored for diffusion codecs that employs patchification and single-step diffusion, reducing computation cost while preserving perceptual quality; (2) at architecture level, we introduce a Cross-level Near Data Processing architecture that integrates the proposed computational universal flash storage (cUFS) and LPDDR-PIM, enabling codecs to be entirely offloaded to data side while reducing visual-data movement and avoiding SoC redesign; (3) at system level, combining both the algorithm and the underlying architecture, we propose a Patch-Workload co-aware scheduling scheme to enable more efficient system-level execution. Experiments show that Dual-stage Diffusion maintains comparable perceptual quality while reducing computation cost by up to 92.7%. Compared with NPU+DRAM and near-data-processing design such as SmartSSD, cUFS+PIM improves coding throughput by 2.32 times and 2.91 times on average, respectively.

不断增长的视觉数据使资源受限边缘设备上的存储不堪重负，因此压缩至关重要。传统编解码器的率失真性能不足，促使人们探索扩散编解码器。扩散编解码器面临两个限制：(1) 在算法层面，扩散解码器在极端压缩下仍能保持感知质量，但需要大量计算。(2) 在架构层面，在以NPU为中心的设备中，神经编解码器占用NPU，干扰其他AI应用。同时，复杂的编解码流水线会产生较长的I/O路径，导致额外开销和能耗。为解决这些问题，我们提出DiffusionSSD，一种面向边缘设备极端视觉数据压缩的跨层级近数据处理框架，采用算法-架构协同设计方法。具体而言，(1) 在算法层面，我们提出双阶段扩散（Dual-stage Diffusion），一种为扩散编解码器量身定制的轻量级解决方案，采用分块化（patchification）和单步扩散，在保持感知质量的同时降低计算成本；(2) 在架构层面，我们引入跨层级近数据处理架构，集成所提出的计算通用闪存存储（cUFS）和LPDDR-PIM，使编解码器能够完全卸载到数据侧，同时减少视觉数据移动并避免SoC重新设计；(3) 在系统层面，结合算法与底层架构，我们提出一种分块-负载协同感知调度方案，以实现更高效的系统级执行。实验表明，双阶段扩散在保持相当感知质量的同时，将计算成本最多降低92.7%。与NPU+DRAM以及SmartSSD等近数据处理设计相比，cUFS+PIM平均分别将编码吞吐量提高2.32倍和2.91倍。

OpenLayout: An Open and Agentic-Driven Platform for Holistic VLSI Physical Design and Benchmarking
OpenLayout：一个开放且由智能体驱动的平台，用于整体VLSI物理设计与基准测试
Qiufeng Li, Sizhe Tang, Quan Cheng, Zirui Wang, Wantong Li, Tian Lan, Weidong Cao
cs.AR
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Physical design automation of very large-scale integrated circuits (VLSI) has made significant strides, progressing from single-stage placement and routing toward increasingly integrated full-flow implementation. Yet, existing approaches struggle with the vast design space optimization (DSO) arising from diverse technology nodes, circuit designs, and varied constraints. The substantial runtime of physical-design tools further exacerbates this challenge, leaving industrial workflows heavily dependent on experienced engineers to navigate the design space and achieve power, performance, and area (PPA) targets, incurring significant engineering effort. We argue that large language models (LLMs) with strong reasoning capabilities, enriched with distilled human design expertise, offer a promising path toward efficient and automated DSO. Unlike traditional algorithms, LLMs can interpret complex design contexts and naturally incorporate human expertise expressed in natural language for reasoning, acting, observing, and adapting throughout the design process, a capability that conventional optimization methods cannot exploit. To this end, we present OpenLayout, an agentic platform that leverages multi-agent collaboration with embedded design knowledge to enable self-iterative DSO. We further introduce OpenLayout-Bench, a benchmark targeting advanced technology nodes with commercial EDA tools to systematically evaluate physical design automation capabilities of LLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that OpenLayout achieves state-of-the-art performance, with approximately 20% accuracy improvement over baseline methods. Our work is open-sourced at https://github.com/fenggev56/OpenLayout

Tracing Knowledge and Reasoning in LLM
追踪大语言模型中的知识与推理
Yang Wang, Xiao Wang, Yiqi Liu, Chenghao Xiao, Eleni Vasilaki, Goran Nenadic, Chenghua Lin
cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Current evaluations of Large Language Models (LLMs) report performance at the dataset or task level, revealing what a model achieves but offering little insight into the internal processing associated with its performance. We propose a task-centric framework for characterising the model-internal processing elicited by a benchmark. It combines attention- and activation-based signals with representation-based measures to characterise benchmarks spanning a continuum from retrieval-dominant to reasoning-dominant and to surface failure modes that task-level scores conceal. We evaluate 16 LLMs across 24 diverse benchmarks and conduct targeted internal-state analyses on representative models and tasks, observing systematic differences in processing between knowledge-centric and reasoning-centric tasks. We shift evaluation from asking what score a model achieves toward characterising the processing patterns associated with that score, potentially supporting more targeted benchmark selection and more informed model choice. We open source our code at https://github.com/extraordinarylab/trace-kr.

cs.CL / 21 / 2611.17754
See Every Block Once: Variance-Reduced Post-Training for Masked Diffusion Language Models
每个块只评估一次：面向掩码扩散语言模型的方差缩减后训练
Yunzhen He, Hiroaki Yamagiwa, Hidetoshi Shimodaira
cs.CL
diffusionlarge language model
扩散模型相关
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Policy gradient methods are widely used to align large language model outputs with human preferences, but the log-likelihoods they require cannot be directly computed in Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs). MDLM post-training therefore relies on a surrogate objective based on the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO), and recent work estimates a blockwise variant of this objective via Monte Carlo methods to support blockwise generation. However, existing estimation schemes randomly sample the block index, so they may evaluate the same block more than once and leave some blocks unevaluated. This introduces variance arising from block selection into the estimates. In this work, we propose a full-block estimator that evaluates every block exactly once and averages the results. We theoretically show that the proposed estimator remains unbiased while eliminating the between-group variance arising from block selection, thereby achieving lower variance than the baseline for the same sample size. In experiments on five benchmarks spanning mathematics, planning, and coding, our method achieves the highest accuracy on four benchmarks and outperforms the random block sampling baseline by an average of 12.79 points.

Activation Steering the Nan-Shan in Typhoon
在《台风》中对南山号进行激活引导
Megan Wei, Damon Pham, John Cayley, Ellie Pavlick
cs.CL · cs.AI · cs.LG · cs.SD
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Typhoon is an audiovisual installation based on Joseph Conrad's novella, in which Captain MacWhirr steers the steamer Nan-Shan directly into and through a catastrophic storm. The novella is a study of what happens when the scale of a phenomenon exceeds the practical and conceptual frameworks available to comprehend it and of the paralysis that sometimes follows a demand for action in such overwhelming circumstances. We chose the text of Typhoon to reflect on human culture's current AI moment. We prompt a large language model, seeded with Conrad's original language, to feed endlessly on its own output, and explore when and how the text becomes increasingly alien and unwieldy as time progresses, including deviations from the narrative, digressions into the model's own instructions and thinking, and long periods of incoherent rumination. We employ methods from activation steering, including a novel adaptive steering method we introduce, to control the rhythm of the narrative and the model's digressions over long stretches of generation. The simultaneous efficacy and unpredictability of this steering showcases the tension between the perceived interpretability of AI, and the reality of a system that remains outside our comprehension and control.

cs.CL / 23 / 2611.17811
AI Reviews Are Biased Towards the Appendix
AI 审稿意见偏向于附录
Saptarshi Ghosh, Mohammad Saim, Tianyu Jiang
cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by reviewers to highlight weaknesses during the reviewing process, and their outputs often shape the reviews that humans submit. Prior work studies how LLM reviews align with human ones, yet we know little about which parts of a paper these models actually criticize. In this work, we investigate 2,000 papers from ACL, EMNLP, COLM, and ICLR, and show that the latest GPT and Claude models draw about 50% or more of their review weaknesses from the appendix. This behavior holds across the API, coding-agent, and web access methods for using LLMs to review a paper. In contrast, we find that humans draw only 8.4% of their weaknesses from the appendix, and these weaknesses primarily revolve around a paper's presentation and content related to its core idea. Meanwhile, LLMs draw weaknesses from deep in the appendix that concern fine-grained technical details not central to the paper's contributions. We anticipate that our analysis will contribute to the scholarly discourse on AI reviews. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/cincynlp/review_appendix.

cs.CL / 24 / 2611.17931
MineralExp: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Experimental Design in Real-World Mineral Exploration
MineralExp：面向真实世界矿产勘探中实验设计的LLM智能体基准测试
Bing Wang, Mingyang Wang, Yuanhang Mi, Yue Xu, Kaibo Su, Li'ang Xiao, Chunguo Wu, Changchun Li, Ximing Li, Kun-Feng Qiu, Bo Yang
cs.CL · cs.AI · cs.CE
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
In mineral exploration, verifying geological hypotheses relies on designing a variety of geochemical experiments. While large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate downstream geoscientific data analysis, a crucial upstream capability remains largely unexplored: automated geochemical experimental design, i.e., deciding which experiments to conduct before committing to costly laboratory work. Evaluating this capability of LLM agents is difficult for two reasons. First, geochemical experiments exhibit high methodological diversity and subjectivity: to test the same hypothesis, different geologists may choose different minerals and instruments, so no single protocol can serve as the reference answer. Second, open-ended experimental plans resist representation as structured labels, which makes objective scoring difficult. To address these challenges, we introduce MineralExp, a benchmark that reformulates geochemical experimental design as a hypothesis-based experiment selection problem, i.e., a multi-label classification problem. We abstract concrete instrument names and procedural details into a shared taxonomy of 19 experimental types across five mineral-system processes: Source, Transport, Deposition, Modification, and Preservation. This taxonomy yields deterministic multi-label targets while accommodating diverse experimental protocols. Constructed from published ore-deposit literature and verified by domain experts, MineralExp comprises 277 cases and 1,572 reference experimental plans. Given a geological hypothesis and initial context, an LLM agent selects the relevant tasks and generates concrete experimental plans, which are scored by multi-label classification metrics and an LLM-as-a-Judge metric. The experimental results show that current agents generally achieve high recall but low precision, tending to propose more experiments than are necessary to test the hypothesis.
Chinese Translation
在矿产勘探中，验证地质假说依赖于设计多种地球化学实验。尽管大语言模型（LLM）智能体日益自动化下游地球科学数据分析，但一项关键的上游能力仍在很大程度上尚未被探索：自动化地球化学实验设计，即在投入昂贵的实验室工作之前决定应开展哪些实验。评估LLM智能体的这一能力存在两方面困难。第一，地球化学实验具有高度的方法多样性和主观性：为检验同一假说，不同地质学家可能选择不同的矿物和仪器，因此没有单一方案可以作为参考答案。第二，开放式的实验方案难以表示为结构化标签，这使得客观评分变得困难。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了MineralExp，一个将地球化学实验设计重新表述为基于假说的实验选择问题（即多标签分类问题）的基准。我们将具体的仪器名称和流程细节抽象为一个共享分类体系，涵盖跨越五个成矿系统过程的19种实验类型：源、运移、沉积、改造和保存。该分类体系在容纳多样化实验方案的同时，产生了确定性的多标签目标。MineralExp基于已发表的矿床文献构建，并经领域专家验证，包含277个案例和1,572个参考实验方案。给定一个地质假说和初始上下文，LLM智能体选择相关任务并生成具体实验方案，这些方案通过多标签分类指标和一个LLM作为评判者的指标进行评分。实验结果表明，当前智能体通常实现高召回率但低精确率，倾向于提出比检验假说所需更多的实验。

cs.CL / 25 / 2611.18157
A Multi-Layered AI Pipeline for BPMN Generation from Natural Language
一种用于从自然语言生成 BPMN 的多层 AI 流水线
Alexandru Rancea, Ionut Anghel, Tudor Cioara, Ovidiu Anchidin, Ancuta Nemes
cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
The automated transformation of unstructured natural language process descriptions into Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) diagrams remains a challenging task, due to linguistic ambiguity and the difficulty to detect non-adjacent control-flow relationships. This paper proposes a solution for generating BPMN models from natural language descriptions through a multi-layered pipeline that integrates transformer-based architectures, Large Language Models and ontology population. The proposed method starts with natural language descriptions and separates textual processing, control-flow relation extraction, and structural validation into dedicated components. Entity extraction is performed using a fine-tuned RoBERTa-based transformer model while a control-flow engine captures non-adjacent relationships through LLM-based inference. To improve generalization under limited available data, the approach uses a dynamic few-shot strategy with con- trolled template input construction, enabling improved generalization under limited data availability. Additionally, an ontology population mechanism is introduced to represent the generated process models as semantic knowledge structures. For evaluation purposes we have used from the literature and industry reference processes. Additionally, we have validated the proposed method on a real-world heart disease patients transitional care process, capturing practical constraints and multi-actor collaboration, and serving as an expert-defined benchmark scenario. Experimental results demonstrate strong performance in the relation extraction task, achieving an accuracy of 90.7%, as well as maintaining structural alignment between the generated and reference BPMN models across multiple evaluation scenarios scenarios.
Chinese Translation
将非结构化自然语言流程描述自动转换为业务流程模型与符号（BPMN）图仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务，原因在于语言歧义以及难以检测非相邻控制流关系。本文提出一种通过多层流水线从自然语言描述生成 BPMN 模型的解决方案，该流水线集成了基于 Transformer 的架构、大型语言模型和本体填充。所提方法从自然语言描述开始，将文本处理、控制流关系抽取和结构验证分离到专门组件中。实体抽取使用经过微调的基于 RoBERTa 的 Transformer 模型执行，而控制流引擎通过基于 LLM 的推理捕获非相邻关系。为在有限可用数据下提高泛化能力，该方法采用动态少样本策略，并配合受控的模板输入构建，从而在有限数据可用性下实现更好的泛化。此外，引入本体填充机制，将生成的流程模型表示为语义知识结构。为了评估，我们使用了文献和行业参考流程。此外，我们在一个真实世界的心脏病患者过渡护理流程上验证了所提方法，该流程捕获了实际约束和多参与者协作，并作为专家定义的基准场景。实验结果表明，该方法在关系抽取任务中表现强劲，达到 90.7% 的准确率，并在多个评估场景中保持生成 BPMN 模型与参考 BPMN 模型之间的结构对齐。

cs.CL / 26 / 2611.18371
Evaluating Human Sensitivity to Gender Biased AI-Generated Content through Human Assessment of Google's Autocomplete Function
通过人类对谷歌自动补全功能的评估来考察人类对性别偏见AI生成内容的敏感性
Tamara Serrano, Nikoleta Pantelidou, Raquel Montero, Natalia Moskvina, Paolo Morosi, Evelina Leivada
cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly present, but their effects on human users remain underexplored. We tested whether AI-attributed information influences views regarding gender-related statements, a domain where biases are pervasive. Spanish-speaking participants recruited through Prolific evaluated biased statements derived from Google's autocomplete function and human-authored, non-biased gender-related facts. Participants were either informed that some of the material was AI-generated or received no information about its origin. AI attribution did not affect reaction times or agreement judgments. This result suggests that AI-generated content does not automatically raise credibility concerns and may be evaluated with less scrutiny compared to human-produced content.
Chinese Translation
大型语言模型（LLMs）日益普遍，但其对人类用户的影响仍缺乏充分探讨。我们检验了归因于AI的信息是否会影响人们对性别相关陈述的看法，而性别相关陈述正是偏见普遍存在的领域。通过Prolific招募的西班牙语参与者评估了源自谷歌自动补全功能的偏见陈述，以及由人类撰写的、无偏见的性别相关事实。参与者或被明确告知部分材料由AI生成，或未获得关于其来源的任何信息。AI归因并未影响反应时或同意判断。这一结果表明，AI生成内容并不会自动引发可信度担忧，并且与人类生产的内容相比，其受到审视的程度可能更低。

cs.CL / 27 / 2611.18506
Agentic induction of common data elements and information extraction from sarcoma case report abstracts
智能体驱动的通用数据元素归纳及肉瘤病例报告摘要的信息抽取
Daniel Reichenpfader, Kwaku Appiagyei, Manovah Nadarajah, Ahmed Reda Bahr, Irina Filchenko, Fabio Dennstädt
cs.CL
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Common data elements (CDEs) support clinical data comparability but are usually defined manually. We investigated whether large language model agents could derive a CDE schema from sarcoma case report abstracts and apply it at scale. An agent proposed 72,909 candidate CDEs from 24,588 PubMed abstracts; agentic consolidation yielded 1,570 CDEs. We retained 53 supported by at least 50 abstracts and attempted evidence-linked extraction across the corpus. Processing succeeded for all retained CDEs in 79.2% of abstracts, including responses indicating non-mention. Two human reviewers approved 83.0% of retained CDEs for relevance, 67.9% for clarity, and 56.6% for correctness. In 100 sampled abstracts, exact agreement with the value extracted by the LLM was 94.9% among 1,116 pairs with a non-empty answer (abstract-level bootstrap 95% CI, 93.0-96.4%). Errors concentrated in anatomical and temporal fields. These findings support agent-assisted schema drafting followed by expert curation before large-scale extraction.
Chinese Translation

cs.CR / 28 / 2611.17534
I2C-GRPO: Insight Importance Checklist for Reinforcement Learning in a Multi-Agent DDoS Alert Triage Workflow
I2C-GRPO：面向多智能体DDoS告警分诊工作流中强化学习的洞见重要性清单
Eyal Zloof, Shaun Shuster, Aviram Zilberman, Chen Frydman, Inderjeet Singh, Andrés Murilo, Asaf Shabtai, Yuval Elovici, Rami Puzis
cs.CR
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for investigative tasks over incomplete evidence, such as cybersecurity alert triage, but reinforcement learning (RL) for such tasks is difficult: intermediate steps have no verifiable labels, rollouts are costly, and outcome rewards are sparse. We propose Insight Importance Checklist (I2C)-GRPO, a checklist reward for intermediate investigation steps. For each decision point, a panel of expert LLMs proposes investigative actions, their atomic forensic insights are extracted, and a separate three-model ranker panel orders the insights by importance. A reward model checks which ranked insights a candidate action targets and returns their consensus-weighted coverage. Because actions are scored without being executed, the reward is a proxy for expected investigative value, and training reduces to a one-step contextual bandit over fixed investigation prefixes. From a soft-computing perspective, the reward is an additive fuzzy measure whose rank-exponent weights act as an ordered weighted averaging (OWA)-type operator; one exponent sets its orness, i.e., how strongly the reward concentrates on the top of the judges' consensus. We train an open-weight Qwen3-4B Triage Agent within a fixed multi-agent Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) alert-triage workflow on DARPA 2000 LLDOS 1.0 and test it on an independent CSE-CIC-IDS2018 DDoS trace. Across three training seeds, the agent reaches a mean F1-score of 0.869, 5.9 and 9.7 points above uniform insight weights and DIVA, and a false-positive rate of 4.9% versus 16.4% for GPT-5-mini. Within the weighting family, false positives fall only at the highest-orness weighting evaluated. All claims are limited to DDoS alert triage; the code, task contexts, and ranked insight dataset will be released.
Chinese Translation
大语言模型（LLM）智能体正越来越多地被用于证据不完整情况下的调查任务，例如网络安全告警分诊，但面向此类任务的强化学习（RL）十分困难：中间步骤没有可验证的标签，轨迹展开（rollout）代价高昂，且结果奖励稀疏。我们提出洞见重要性清单（Insight Importance Checklist，I2C）-GRPO，一种面向中间调查步骤的清单奖励。对于每个决策点，由一组专家LLM提出调查动作，提取其原子化的取证洞见，并由一个独立的三模型排序器小组按重要性对洞见进行排序。奖励模型检查候选动作针对的是哪些已排序洞见，并返回其共识加权覆盖率。由于动作是在未被执行的情况下被评分的，该奖励是预期调查价值的代理，训练因而归结为在固定调查前缀上的一步上下文赌博机问题。从软计算的角度看，该奖励是一种可加模糊测度，其秩指数权重充当有序加权平均（OWA）型算子；其中一个指数设定其orness，即奖励在多大程度上集中于评审共识的顶部。我们在固定的多智能体分布式拒绝服务（DDoS）告警分诊工作流内，在DARPA 2000 LLDOS 1.0上训练了一个开放权重的Qwen3-4B分诊智能体，并在独立的CSE-CIC-IDS2018 DDoS轨迹上进行测试。在三个训练随机种子上，该智能体达到平均F1分数0.869，分别比均匀洞见权重和DIVA高出5.9和9.7个百分点，假阳性率为4.9%，而GPT-5-mini为16.4%。在权重族内，假阳性仅在所评估的最高orness加权下才下降。所有结论均限于DDoS告警分诊；代码、任务上下文和已排序洞见数据集将予以发布。

cs.CR / 29 / 2611.17818
LLMs are Vulnerable to Malicious Requests with Indistinguishable Intent
大型语言模型易受意图不可区分的恶意请求影响
Wenzhuo Xu, Zonghao Ying, Deyue Zhang, Dongdong Yang, Xiangzheng Zhang, Quanchen Zou
cs.CR
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks can circumvent the safety mechanisms of Large Language Models (LLMs) and lead to the generation of harmful or policy-violating content. We first examine eight jailbreak methods against an LLM-based filter and observe that requests resembling normal user queries tend to have higher filter pass rates than those containing obvious anomalous patterns. This observation motivates the hypothesis that resemblance to normal requests may contribute to jailbreak success. We introduce \textbf{Detail Guidance Attack (DGA)}, a framework that uses an auxiliary LLM to construct detailed requests and interacts with the victim model in a single turn. Across 11 victim models and three datasets, the complete DGA pipeline achieves high attack success rates under our automated judging criterion, including rates above 95\% in several evaluated settings. We also evaluate a DGA-aware filter and conduct a user study on everyday requests with ambiguous or dual-use intent. The survey documents differing expectations about whether models should respond; the usability costs of the filter remain to be measured.
Chinese Translation
越狱攻击能够绕过大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全机制，并导致生成有害或违反政策的内容。我们首先针对一个基于 LLM 的过滤器考察了八种越狱方法，并观察到，与包含明显异常模式的请求相比，类似普通用户查询的请求往往具有更高的过滤器通过率。这一观察促使我们提出假设：与正常请求的相似性可能有助于越狱成功。我们引入细节引导攻击（Detail Guidance Attack，DGA），这是一个使用辅助 LLM 构造详细请求并在单轮中与受害模型交互的框架。在 11 个受害模型和三个数据集上，完整的 DGA 流程在我们自动评判标准下取得了高攻击成功率，其中包括在若干评估设置中超过 95% 的成功率。我们还评估了一个 DGA 感知过滤器，并针对具有模糊意图或双重用途意图的日常请求开展了一项用户研究。该调查记录了人们对于模型是否应当作出回应的不同期望；该过滤器的可用性成本仍有待测量。

cs.CR / 30 / 2611.17918
Condensing Law of Security Information
安全信息的凝聚定律
Qi Tan, Tianyu Cui, Qi Li, Ke Xu
cs.CR · cs.AI · cs.LG
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Scaling laws indicate that the generalization error of a large language model (LLM) decreases monotonically with model size once data is sufficient, and the corollary that larger models outperform smaller ones is now widely accepted. We argue that this corollary fails in security: decisions there hinge on anomalous inputs, which are long-tailed (e.g., zero-day attacks) and therefore poorly served by likelihood fitting. Worse still, the capability brought about by scale (e.g., inferring the intended whole from a corrupted part) leads a LLM silently \emph{repair} the anomaly before any downstream security head ever sees it. We formalize this as a tradeoff between two quantities: the cognitive capability $K(W)=I(W;P)$ that a model holds about the world distribution, and the security-information retention $R(W)=\frac{I(Z;S)}{I(A;S)}$ that survives in its intermediate representation. We derive three information-theoretic bounds to connect them: an upper bound on restoration accuracy in terms of $K(W)$, an upper bound on $R(W)$ in terms of the restoration error, and a Fano lower bound on security-task error in terms of $R(W)$. These theorems approximately imply that usable security information is a product of how much security information survives and how well it can be extracted, indicating that optimal usable security information can be achieved at a certain model size. We state this as the \emph{condensing law of security information}: usable security information is unimodal in model size, so security generalization error is U-shaped and is minimized at a finite $N^{\star}$ rather than decreasing without bound. The experiments on various real-world LLMs validate the condensing law, indicating that there is an optimal model size to solve security issues.
Chinese Translation
缩放定律表明，一旦数据充足，大语言模型（LLM）的泛化误差会随模型规模单调下降；而“更大模型优于更小模型”这一推论如今已被广泛接受。我们认为，这一推论在安全领域并不成立：安全决策依赖于异常输入，而异常输入具有长尾分布（例如零日攻击），因此似然拟合难以为其提供良好支持。更糟的是，规模带来的能力（例如从受损部分推断出预期整体）会导致 LLM 在任何下游安全头看到异常之前就悄然\emph{修复}该异常。我们将此形式化为两个量之间的权衡：模型关于世界分布所拥有的认知能力 $K(W)=I(W;P)$，以及在其中间表示中得以保留的安全信息保留度 $R(W)=\frac{I(Z;S)}{I(A;S)}$。我们推导出三个信息论界来将二者联系起来：以 $K(W)$ 表示的恢复准确率上界、以恢复误差表示的 $R(W)$ 上界，以及以 $R(W)$ 表示的安全任务误差的 Fano 下界。这些定理近似地意味着，可用安全信息是“有多少安全信息得以保留”与“其能被多好地提取”的乘积，这表明在某一模型规模下可以实现最优的可用安全信息。我们将其表述为\emph{安全信息的凝聚定律}：可用安全信息随模型规模呈单峰变化，因此安全泛化误差呈 U 形，并在有限的 $N^{\star}$ 处达到最小，而不是无界下降。在多种真实世界 LLM 上的实验验证了该凝聚定律，表明存在一个解决安全问题的最优模型规模。

cs.CR / 31 / 2611.18133
Forget the Lineage: Forward-Secure, Lineage-Complete Cryptographic Erasure for LLM-Agent Memory
忘记谱系：面向 LLM 智能体记忆的前向安全、谱系完整密码学擦除
Yulliwas Ameur, Billal Mokhtari, Samia Bouzefrane
cs.CR
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents keep a long-term memory that the model itself writes: summaries, extracted facts and embeddings derived from what users said. Deleting a message is therefore not enough: its derivatives, storage residue and backups survive. We define lineage-complete forward-secure erasure: after a forget request, an adversary that later obtains all storage and the agent's current keys learns nothing about the forgotten input or any memory derived from it. We present LinEra, which encrypts each memory under a key derived from its parents' keys along the derivation graph; source keys come from a puncturable pseudorandom function, so forgetting is a single puncture and every descendant becomes unreadable without being located. A sound lineage rule records as parents everything the writer saw, which confines all influence of a source to its descendants. We prove security and lineage integrity and integrate LinEra with mem0. With real LLMs from two families (1.5B to 7B parameters) writing the memory of LoCoMo conversations, an attacker holding every file and the current keys recovers, on all ten conversations written by the largest model, 70 of 71 forgotten secrets after logical deletion, 67 with per-object or epoch keys, and none with LinEra; the same holds for every writer tested. The price is a larger forgetting radius, which follows a simple law in the writer's fan-in: forgetting 1% of turns erases 73.0% of derived memories with session-level summaries but only 3.8% with four-turn windows, which also answer better; re-deriving restores what is lost.
Chinese Translation
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体维护着一种由模型自身写入的长期记忆：摘要、抽取的事实以及由用户所说内容派生出的嵌入。因此，删除一条消息并不够：它的派生内容、存储残留和备份仍会存活。我们定义了谱系完整的前向安全擦除：在收到遗忘请求之后，一个后来获得全部存储和智能体当前密钥的敌手，无法获知关于被遗忘输入或由其所派生出的任何记忆的任何信息。我们提出 LinEra，它沿派生图将每条记忆加密在其父记忆密钥所派生出的密钥之下；源密钥来自可穿刺伪随机函数，因此遗忘就是一次穿刺，所有后代都变得不可读，而无需定位它们。一条可靠的谱系规则将写入者所见的一切都记录为父节点，从而将某个来源的全部影响限制在其后代之中。我们证明了安全性和谱系完整性，并将 LinEra 与 mem0 集成。使用来自两个家族（1.5B 至 7B 参数）的真实 LLM 写入 LoCoMo 对话的记忆时，一个持有每个文件和当前密钥的攻击者，在由最大模型写入的全部十段对话上，在逻辑删除后恢复了 71 个被遗忘秘密中的 70 个，在使用按对象密钥或纪元密钥时恢复了 67 个，而在使用 LinEra 时一个也恢复不了；这对所测试的每个写入者都成立。代价是更大的遗忘半径，它遵循写入者扇入中的一个简单规律：遗忘 1% 的轮次时，使用会话级摘要会擦除 73.0% 的派生记忆，而使用四轮窗口时只擦除 3.8%，后者的回答效果也更好；重新派生可以恢复丢失的内容。

From Bytes to Behavior: Request-Level Attribution from Live GPU Hidden States
从字节到行为：来自实时 GPU 隐藏状态的请求级归因
Jonathan Pan, Yidi Yuan, Xin Wei Chia, Xinhui Su, Vincent Tan, Brenda Ong
cs.CR · cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Existing memory forensics for machine learning systems typically targets static artifacts, such as model weights or prompt logs, rather than the transient computational states of a live inference process. This gap limits an examiner's ability to analyze the specific behavior of a running large language model (LLM) service that cannot be restarted or modified. We present a method for acquiring per-layer residual-stream activations from a live GPU process using a non-modifying debugger, without requiring prior knowledge of the model's architecture parameters or weights. The approach employs a general six-stage process with platform-specific adapters, utilizing either a PyTorch allocator clock or a kernel-launch clock to identify and read tensor allocations from video memory. We validate this acquisition on 33 open-source decoder-only models across nine architecture classes, demonstrating that the recovered bytes are byte-exact and usable for downstream analysis. As a use case, we apply linear probes trained on standard framework activations to the recovered states, successfully distinguishing refusal from compliance with an AUROC of 0.88 on held-out prompts. This work establishes that live, observational access to intermediate hidden states is feasible on named inference platforms, providing a new source of evidence for examining the operational behavior of deployed AI systems.

Training-Free Acceleration of Multi-View 3D Transformers via Regime-Aware Attention Approximation
基于区制感知注意力近似的多视图3D Transformer免训练加速
Muhammed Izzet Sezer, Sevval Belkis Dikkaya
cs.CV · cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Multi-view 3D transformers such as VGGT reconstruct a scene from a set of unposed images in a single forward pass, predicting camera poses, depth and 3D points, but the cost of their global attention grows quadratically with the num- ber of images. Accelerators borrowed from large language models assume that each token attends to only a few oth- ers; on these models they lose accuracy or gain little speed. We study the global attention of three multi-view transform- ers on seven datasets and find that every layer plays one of three roles: it averages over the whole scene, matches points across many images, or anchors on the first image, whose coordinates the outputs use. Building on this anal- ysis, we propose Regime-AWare ATTention Approximation (RAWATTA), a training-free method that runs attention in 8- bit precision and keeps 16-bit precision only in the layers where a worst-case calibration check requires it. Its fast set- ting, RAWATTA-Fast, also approximates attention in a few averaging and anchoring layers, while matching layers and the first image keep all their keys. On a 12 GB GPU at 50 im- ages, RAWATTA runs VGGT faster than the released code, mostly through our CUDA engine, and loses at most about one point of pose accuracy (AUC@3) on any dataset; RAWATTA-Fast is slightly faster and less accurate. Both fit 200 images, where the released code runs out of memory at 100.

VGGT 等多视图3D Transformer 能在单次前向传播中从一组无位姿图像重建场景，预测相机位姿、深度和3D点，但其全局注意力的代价随图像数量呈二次增长。借用自大语言模型的加速器假设每个 token 只关注少数其他 token；在这些模型上，它们会损失精度或几乎不能带来速度提升。我们在七个数据集上研究三个多视图 Transformer 的全局注意力，发现每一层都扮演三种角色之一：在整个场景上做平均、在许多图像之间匹配点，或以第一张图像为锚，而输出使用的正是该图像的坐标。基于这一分析，我们提出区制感知注意力近似（Regime-AWare ATTention Approximation，RAWATTA），这是一种免训练方法，以8位精度运行注意力，并且仅在最坏情况校准检查要求它的层中保留16位精度。其快速设置 RAWATTA-Fast 还在少数平均层和锚定层中近似注意力，而匹配层和第一张图像保留其全部键。在50张图像、12 GB GPU 上，RAWATTA 运行 VGGT 比已发布代码更快，主要通过我们的 CUDA 引擎实现，并且在任何数据集上最多损失约一个点的位姿精度（AUC@3）；RAWATTA-Fast 略快一些，精度也略低。两者都能容纳200张图像，而已发布代码在100张图像时就内存不足。

Can Large Language Models Optimize Pretrained Neural Network Weights? A Multi-Phase Empirical Evaluation of Gradient-Free Editing in YOLO26 Object Detection
大语言模型能否优化预训练神经网络权重？YOLO26 目标检测中无梯度编辑的多阶段实证评估
Mohamed Abdallah Salem
cs.CV · cs.AI
large language model
大语言模型相关
Abstract
Direct weight editing offers a gradient-free alternative to fine-tuning pretrained detectors, but its generalization and the value of large language model (LLM) guidance remain uncertain. We evaluate five experimental phases using YOLO26 models and MS COCO, comparing deterministic scaling, random perturbations, differential evolution, CMA-ES, structured interventions, real local-LLM proposals, failure-aware guidance, and hybrid LLM-CMA-ES against a matched random-policy control. Optimization uses native Ultralytics mAP50-95 on 1,000 COCO Val2017 images; selected candidates are assessed on a disjoint, previously examined 1,500-image subset, with COCO API AP50-95 reported separately where available. Increasing the CMA-ES budget from 8 to 250 evaluations raised development gains from +0.11 to +0.28 percentage points, while selection changes remained negative (-0.03 to -0.12 points); the 250-evaluation result uses one seed. The hybrid LLM approach showed no statistically established advantage over random-policy guidance (p = 0.612, 0.369, and 0.694 at budgets 8, 25, and 50). Five-seed YOLO26m replication yielded a COCO API AP difference of +0.004 points (95% CI: -0.026 to +0.034). Of 225 archived LLM proposals, 53 satisfy the final strict schema; early tensor-level execution cannot be fully verified. Genuine LLM-mediated weight editing is feasible, but the tested approaches did not demonstrate consistent transfer beyond the development subset or a measurable LLM advantage.

JaRS: Contrast-Free Localization of Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Jacobian Row Sums
JaRS：通过雅可比行和实现扩散模型中记忆区域的无对比定位
Saisuchith Mahajan, Aditey Nandan, Rohit Singh, Nishanth Shetty, Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula
cs.CV · cs.LG
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Diffusion models sometimes memorize and reproduce regions of their training images, raising privacy and copyright concerns. We aim to localize these regions. Existing contrast-based methods use the diagonal of the denoiser Jacobian, which reflects the posterior variance of each pixel. However, a small diagonal entry is ambiguous: it signals either memorization or averaging over many neighboring pixels in smooth or predictable regions. To resolve this ambiguity, these methods subtract a reference map, which requires a second evaluation with a null prompt or an earlier checkpoint. Unconditional models have no null prompt, and earlier checkpoints are unavailable in general. We show that Jacobian row sums (JaRS), unlike the diagonal, separate memorization from local averaging without a reference map. Pairing JaRS with an image-level detection score that uses the same statistic at an early denoising step yields JaRS+. Across Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v2.1, Realistic Vision v5.1, and an unconditional diffusion Transformer (DiT), JaRS+ outperforms contrast-based localization methods.

TelePhysica: Agentic Reconstruction of Physically Interactive Scenes
TelePhysica：物理交互场景的智能体重建
Ruiqi Shu, Xufeng Zhan, Yuanzhi Liang, Haibin Huang, Chi Zhang, Xuelong Li
cs.CV · cs.AI
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Direct image-to-video diffusion models entangle pixel synthesis with implicit latent motion, precluding persistent 3D mechanical equilibrium, mid-rollout intervention, and counterfactual force control. Conversely, single-pass programmatic 3D reconstruction from an uncalibrated photograph leaves monocular depth and support clearance underconstrained, causing reconstructed scenes to interpenetrate or collapse under gravity. We present TelePhysica, a closed-loop framework that reconstructs an executable, multi-regime physical world from a single photograph to drive stateful 3D interaction and trajectory-grounded video synthesis. A vision-language coding agent synthesizes separable visual meshes and declarative physical models within a two-stage verification loop that pairs pixel-aligned silhouette critique with a zero-force gravitational equilibrium preflight. The verified world compiles into a unified solver coupling rigid bodies, articulated chains, co-dimensional cloth shells, and 3D tetrahedral elastodynamics, whose simulated rollouts condition pretrained video diffusion models through pixel-aligned structural streams. On a 100-scene single-image benchmark, closed-loop execution raises equilibrium-verified world recovery from $0\%$ in single-pass synthesis to $81\%$. Controlled constitutive ablations show over an order-of-magnitude larger localized corner displacement and out-of-plane pleating in finite-element cloth than in matched rigid-plate proxies. Conditioning video diffusion on simulated trajectories increases dynamic motion-mask overlap by $2{\times}\text{--}5{\times}$ and preserves motion direction under counterfactual force reversals where direct image-to-video models fail.

直接的图像到视频扩散模型将像素合成与隐式潜运动纠缠在一起，从而使持续的三维力学平衡、推演中途干预以及反事实力控制无法实现。相反，从一张未标定照片进行单遍程序化三维重建会使单目深度与支撑间隙处于欠约束状态，导致重建场景相互穿插或在重力作用下坍塌。我们提出 TelePhysica，这是一个闭环框架，它从单张照片重建可执行的多物理机制物理世界，以驱动有状态的三维交互和轨迹锚定的视频合成。一个视觉-语言编码智能体在两阶段验证循环内合成可分离的视觉网格与声明式物理模型，该循环将像素对齐的轮廓评判与零力重力平衡预检配对。经验证的世界被编译为一个统一求解器，耦合刚体、铰接链、同维布料壳和三维四面体弹性动力学，其模拟推演通过像素对齐的结构流对预训练视频扩散模型进行条件化。在包含 100 个场景的单图像基准上，闭环执行将经平衡验证的世界恢复率从单遍合成中的 $0\%$ 提升至 $81\%$。受控本构消融表明，与匹配的刚性板代理相比，有限元布料中的局部角点位移和面外褶皱大一个数量级以上。以模拟轨迹对视频扩散进行条件化，可使动态运动掩码重叠增加 $2{\times}\text{--}5{\times}$，并在直接图像到视频模型失败的反事实力反转下保持运动方向。

基于动态形状控制的AI多视图超声心动图图像合成
João Freitas, Anca Balinisteanu, Ahmed Youssef, Vitor Hugo Pereira, Jan D'hooge, Sandro Queirós
cs.CV · cs.AI
diffusion
扩散模型相关
Abstract
Although automated methods have improved echocardiographic workflows, progress remains constrained by the need for large, well-annotated datasets. As the development of clinical databases is often limited by ethical and logistical challenges, synthetic data generation has emerged as a promising alternative. However, existing approaches commonly rely on left ventricle shape-based supervision or are tailored to specific cardiac views, limiting the generation of additional standard and unconventional imaging planes and the preservation of multi-view consistency across cardiac chambers. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for shape-controlled synthesis of multi-view echocardiographic sequences. First, multi-view cross-sections are extracted from dynamic whole-heart meshes. Ultrasound-like appearance is then introduced through a two-stage AI-based pipeline. Overall, the proposed framework enables the generation of realistic multi-view echocardiographic sequences from diverse cardiac models, providing a scalable source of annotated data for dataset augmentation and the development of multi-view cardiac assessment methods.
